TextEntrySetState_434410
# Functions COMPILED FROM C into the byte-identical link.
#
# Each must be 100% on the board first -- link.py refuses a slot whose
# compiled .text is the wrong LENGTH, because that shifts every byte
# after it and the report becomes a 3 MB diff with no information in it.
# The image staying byte-identical is what proves the conversion.
#
# scripts/link.py --control keeps every slot in assembly: the arm that
# shows the splitting itself moves no bytes.
# Staged by the concurrent second session (config/cunits_pending.txt) and
# admitted here after the nm check: one T, one .text, no mangled undefineds.
# kill is 8-ALIGNED (0x00116D78), the first unit that is. mwcc gives .text
# sh_addralign 16 and mwld honours it, so this used to shift the image and
# textsplit.py refused the cut. link.py's relax_text_align() now lowers the
# compiled object's .text alignment to what the address actually needs.
# The asymmetric guard pair, closed as a class by the shared-label recipe.
# libvu0, written as the inline assembly it is (see the source files).
# Tier-1 ring picks (third session).
# Tier-1 ring picks, fourth session. Eleven of the twelve matched; the
# twelfth, MemStackReturnToOwnerMaybe_14dad0, is a 3-word scheduling residue written up in its
# own source. CameraModeInsectBattleUpdate_307ac0 is the first unit here whose callee is NOT in the
# main image: func_4F2100 is an overlay entry defined absolutely in the lcf.
# Tier-1 ring picks, fourth session, second batch. Eleven of twelve matched;
# MotionListGetPlayerState_1723a0 is a loop-rotation residue written up in its own source.
# Two of these are C++ because a VIRTUAL call is the only thing that emits
# retail's `lw t9 / lw t9 / jalr t9` -- a struct of function pointers gives
# `jalr v0`. Neither emits a vtable or a mangled symbol.
# Tier-1 ring picks, fourth session, third batch. TWELVE of twelve matched.
# Seven are VU0 macro mode written as inline asm (COP2 is not expressible in
# C); five are the asymmetric-guard-pair class, which the ObjRecTableGet_1c3f60 recipe
# closed unchanged at four new sites.
# ÃƒÂ¢Ã…Â¡Ã‚Â  sceVu0UnitMatrix at 0x0010ABE8 is 8-ALIGNED -- link.py's relax_text_align
# handles it, as it does for `kill`.
# Tier-1 ring picks, fourth session, fourth batch. Nine of nine matched.
# Tier-1 ring picks, fourth session, fifth batch. Five of seven matched;
# ObjGetAttachNodePos_1adec0 (2 words of register naming) and MemStackNew_14e230 (2 words, a
# block mwcc merges and retail does not) are residues written up in-file.
# Sixth batch. WrapAngle_13c050 (17 words of FPR naming from one CSE),
# MemStackNew_14e1b0 (the byte-identical twin of MemStackNew_14e230, same 2-word residue)
# are written up in-file and stay out.
# ==========================================================================
# MERGED 2026-08-23 from config/pending/w1.txt: 3,455 of the fragment's 3,489.
# THIRTY-FOUR ARE HELD BACK and are listed at the end of this block with the
# reason -- they are in config/units.txt (in scope, on the board) but NOT
# compiled into the link:
#    16  a `size:` attribute that swallows trailing padding.  The unit is
#        byte-identical; the RECORDED SIZE is wrong, so link.py would refuse
#        the slot as the wrong length.  Fixing it is a re-split, which moves
#        scores for functions nobody touched -- board-owner action, not a
#        merge action.
#     9  a real residue (2 bad words, or a length mismatch)
#     8  an address that is 8-but-not-16 aligned
#     1  absent from config/main.symbols.txt (bytecheck drops it silently)
# ==========================================================================
# ==========================================================================
# PASS 17, 2026-08-23: 370 units, every one admitted on all FIVE conditions
# (bytecheck MATCH, exact retail size, no own .rodata/.data/.sdata, one T /
# one .text / no mangled undefined, 16-aligned).  build/w1_admit.py checks
# them; the three the board cannot see are the ones that matter.
# The levers: gen15 taking SINGLE-branch functions (--minbr 1, +210), the
# likely branch admitted to the if-else shape (+81), gen13 --calls 6 (+54).
# ==========================================================================
